            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">For an aligned image of height `H` and width `W`, the code grid is</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      91
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">`H&#x2F;32 × W&#x2F;32`. Each grid position contains one code index per quantization</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      92
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">group. Increasing the group count increases the code payload while keeping</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      93
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">the number of spatial positions unchanged.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      94
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn"><br></span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      95
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">### Code-Conditioned Refiner (G16R1, G16R52)</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      96
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn"><br></span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      97
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">The Refiner conditions directly on **discrete codebook embeddings** through</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      98
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">global context tokens and time-conditioned spatial residuals. It requires no</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">

    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">

        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">

    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">

        </div>
    </td>

    </td>

        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      102
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">

        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">

    </td>

        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">

    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">

    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      106
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">  evaluation; the 52-step schedule stored in its configuration defines the</span>

    </td>
</tr><tr>

        </div>
    </td>

        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      209
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">  for G16, G16R1, and G16R52; `G = 64` with values in `[0, 4095]` for G64.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      210
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">- `encoded.out_grid_thw`: `(B, 3)` token-grid dimensions `[1, grid_h, grid_w]`.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      211
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">- `encoded.codebook_quant`: codebook-space features of shape `(N, 4096)`.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      212
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">- `encoded.quants`: decoder-projected features of shape `(N, 1152)`.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      213
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn"><br></span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      214
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">For G16 and G64, `decode` returns ViT pixel-decoder reconstructions at</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      215
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">`(grid_h * 32, grid_w * 32)` in height and width. For G16R1 and G16R52,</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      216
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">`decode` reconstructs the codebook embeddings from the IDs, runs the Refiner,</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      217
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">and returns images at `(2 * grid_h * 32, 2 * grid_w * 32)`.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      218
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn"><br></span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      219
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">For multiple images, `encode` packs their token sequences together. Split the</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      220
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">IDs using each image&#x27;s `grid_h * grid_w` before decoding images with different</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      221
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">grid sizes; `forward` already handles this unpacking.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      222
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn"><br></span>
        </div>
    </td>

      223
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">### Precision and memory</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      224
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn"><br></span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      225
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">**G16 and G64.** The export contains BF16 linear&#x2F;convolution weights and FP32</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      226
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">codebook, normalization, and embedding tensors. CUDA inference uses internal</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      227
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">BF16 autocast. Use the default loading dtype; explicitly requesting</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      228
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">`torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16` also casts FP32 tensors, including codebooks, and</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      229
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">can change discrete token assignments through rounding.</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      230
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn"><br></span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">
      231
    </td>
    <td class="d2h-ins">
        <div class="d2h-code-side-line">
            <span class="d2h-code-line-prefix">+</span>
            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">**G16R1 and G16R52.** Load these Refiner exports with</span>
        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">

    </td>

        </div>
    </td>

        </div>
    </td>
</tr><tr>
    <td class="d2h-code-side-linenumber d2h-ins">

            <span class="d2h-code-line-ctn">Scores are macro-averages over the three official instance-scale buckets.</span>
